    desc = wet_decision_no_description
    icon = generic_sabotage
    allowed = {
      tag = G24
    }
    visible = {

        }
      }

            113 = {
              owner = {
                wet_grn_chaos_dwarf = yes

              }

              any_neighbor_state = {
                is_fully_controlled_by = G24
              }
              NOT = {
                has_state_flag = wet_grn_raid_done
              }
            }
            113 = {
              owner = {
                is_in_array = {
                  G24.wet_grn_raid_113_owner = THIS
                }
              }
            }
          }
          113 = {
            owner = {
              PREV = {
                add_dynamic_modifier = {
                  modifier = wet_grn_slave_sabotage
                  scope = PREV

                }
              }
              country_event = {

          }

            }
          }
        }
